===== [p258-260] Appendix B ASSIST BUTTONS AND THEIR FUNCTIONS (12块) =====
--[p258]-- Item | Function
Break | Continues to suspend a job cascade execution when pressing the <Break> button tostop a job cascade while unloading final wafer of the previous job.
Cancel | Cancels the execution of processing.
Condition | Changes exposure condition.
Continue* | Continues processing or setting of measurement marks by manual operation.
EGA* | Executes from EGA.
End | Ends processing.
Exposure | Executes the exposure.
Go | Starts wafer processing in the READY state in GEM.
--[p259]-- Item | Function
OF retry | Returns the wafer on the wafer stage to the wafer pre-alignment unit_1 and restartsprocessing from OF detection.
OK* | Notifies MCSV that the message has been confirmed or judged to be OK.
Option | Displays the additional assist buttons.
Pass* | Aborts the current mark measurement and proceeds to the next mark. The abortedmark is not used in calculations.In FIA-EGA XY simultaneous measurement, the X axis and Y axis are simultaneouslyexcluded.
Pilot | Executes re-pilot exposure.
--[p258]-- Item | Function
The results of manually setting measurement marks are handledin the same way as those for <Proceed>.
Auto meas. | When performing vernier measurement in "Measurement Assist in Error" mode or"Measurement Assist" mode, all subsequent shots are automatically processed ("NoMeasurement Assist") without manually setting the measurement marks. The results ofmanually setting measurement marks are handled in the same way as when <Proceed>is selected.
--[p259]-- Item | Function
Proceed* | Proceeds to the next process. Proceeds to the next mark in EGA and wafermeasurement. Errors are ignored unless manual operations are executed. When settingmeasurement marks with manual operation, its results are used as the alignmentresults.
Proceed (all)* | Ignores an error and proceeds to the next process. The error is ignored if it occursagain in the same command.
Reject | Rejects the wafer being processed, and processes the next wafer.
--[p260]-- Item | Function
T axis | Displays the waveform of the measured θ mark.
Unload | Depending on the situation, any of the following functions is available.- Returns a reticle to its reticle case.- Unloads a wafer and processes the next wafer.- Interrupts a cascade execution after unloading the wafer which is being unloaded.
Wait | Resumes processing after waiting until processing is made possible.
X1 axis/X2 axis | Displays the waveform of the measured X1 mark or X2 mark.
--[p259]-- Item | Function
Manual | Executes the currently suspended process with manual operation. When manualoperation is completed, the assist buttons reappear.
Manual X | Sets measurement marks in X search alignment with manual operation. When manualsetting of measurement marks is completed, the assist buttons reappear.
Manual Y-T | Sets measurement marks in Y-θ search alignment with manual operation. When manualsetting of measurement marks is completed, the assist buttons reappear.
--[p258]-- After manual operations have been performed in such cases as when errors occur, and when specification of measurement marks is complete, a series of buttons are displayed at the bottom of the error window. These are used for the operator to judge the processing results and to select the next processing function.
The operator uses these buttons to select the processing item appropriate to the particular situation.
--[p260]-- Item | Function
X search* | Executes from X-search alignment.
Y axis | Displays the waveform of the measured Y mark.
Yes | Responds "Yes" to the contents of the confirmation message.
Y search | Executes from Y-search alignment.
This button is not displayed on errors where "Protect" is specified by assist protection of the operator data. Concerning operator data, see Chapter 22, "AUTHORIZE Command," in "NSR/MCSV Command Explanation (Part VI SYSTEM Group)."
This page intentionally left blank.
NIKON CORPORATION Fuji bldg. 3-2-3 Marunouchi, Chiyoda-ku, Tokyo, JAPAN 100-8331 TEL.81-3-3216-1004

Table: Assist Buttons and Their Functions (1/3)
Item | Function
Abort | Aborts the command.
Auto EGA | When performing EGA in "Manual EGA" mode or "EGA Assist" mode, all subsequentEGA shots are automatically processed (in "Auto EGA" mode) without manually settingmeasurement marks.
--[p258 [表]]-- Item | Function
JOB Abort | Ends a job cascade execution.
Table: Assist Buttons and Their Functions (2/3)
Item | Function
Lot pass | Transports the wafer remained in the interrupted lot to unload destination withoutexecuting exposure and restart the job cascade execution from the next job.
Lot reject | Transports the wafer remained in the interrupted lot to reject destination withoutexecuting exposure and restart the job cascade execution from the next job.
Main AF | Performs focusing by main AF sensor.
--[p259 [表]]-- Item | Function
Restart | Re-executes a process or setting of measurement marks by manual operation.
Retry | Retries the currently suspended process. Normally, select <Retry> after settingmeasurement marks by manual operation when an error occurs.
Shift | Performs shift focusing or shift leveling. The assist buttons reappear after eitherprocessing is complete.
Signal | Displays the signal waveforms obtained from measurement.

--[p259]-- Item | Function
Manual Z | Executes focusing with manual operation. When manual operation is completed, theassist buttons are displayed again.

next seq. | Displays the assist buttons for selecting the next process (such as EGA and Y search).
NG | Judges the contents of the confirmation message displayed in the window to be NG.
No | Responds "No" to the contents of the confirmation message displayed in the window.

===== [p3-4] WARNING (6块) =====
--[p3]-- The high power Hg lamp and the halogen lamp can explode or burst if its surface is stained with fingerprints or other dirt, or if it receives a physical shock.
Always wear clean-room gloves when handling the high power Hg lamp and the halogen lamp. Also, be cautious not to apply a physical shock to the lamp.
--[p4]-- Prolonged use of a keyboard and monitor may cause fatigue. Take periodic rests and maintain proper posture when operating the keyboard.
--[p3]-- Laser radiation will cause damage if it hits the eye directly.
Always wear laser safety goggles to prevent eye exposure from di rect or reflected laser radiation.
--[p4]-- When replacing the high power Hg lamp and the halogen lamp, first turn off its control unit and wait for at least 30 minutes. Check that the lamp has cooled down before proceeding.
--[p3]-- The high power Hg lamp operates on high voltage and generates ultraviolet rays. Direct exposure to this radiation can cause eye and skin damage.
Wear eye and skin protection to safeguard yourself from the ultraviolet laser radiation and its intense brightness.
--[p4]-- • Potentially life-threatening high voltage applies throughout the system.
• Do not touch any power supplies or lead wires (especially lead wires connected to the lamp.)
• Observe safety labels and standard electrical safety precautions to avoid personal injury or damage to the equipment.
To prevent injury, do not let hands, feet, hair, or clothing get caught in moving parts. Physical injury may result.

===== [p257-257] [8] Additional Message (1块) =====
--[p257]-- This is the message providing additional information regarding the state in which the error occurred as well as other information. There are cases where additional information is not provided.

===== [p3-3] CAUTION (2块) =====
--[p3]-- • WARNING indicates that personal harm is possible if the warning is not heeded.
• CAUTION indicates that the system may be damaged if the caution is not heeded.
• NOTES contain additional information and requests to the user.
The following is a listing of WARNINGS, CAUTIONS, and NOTES that should be observed throughout NSR system operation.
--[p3]-- Before operating the system, read this information thoroughly, and approach the performance of operations only after a sufficient understanding of its contents has been attained.
In an effort to protect users from personal harm and to protect the system from damage, Nikon has adopted the use of WARNING, CAUTION, and NOTES in this manual.
• WARNING indicates that personal harm is possible if the warning is not heeded.

===== [p257-257] NOTE (1块) =====
--[p257]-- In the GEM environment under NSR/GOCS, the alarm is reported to the host computer using a system code [4] as an alarm ID (ALID).

===== [p5-5] (3) Notes (2块) =====
--[p5]-- • When replacing the high power Hg lamp and the halogen lamp, always wear clean-room gloves.
If the surface of the lamp should be stained with dirt, wipe it off with the miracle clean cloth soaked with the alcohol.
--[p5]-- • For the maintenance that is not described in the operation manual, please contact Nikon.
• Be sure to back up the system disks and user data disks.
Backup enables the system to restore in a short time when a serious disk error occurs.
• In order to ensure the safety of the equipment, use the repair parts provided by Nikon. For the repair parts, please contact Nikon.
• When replacing the high power Hg lamp and the halogen lamp, always wear clean-room gloves.

===== [p2-2] PREFACE (1块) =====
--[p2]-- This error code list is the collection of information related to error codes for the NSR-2205i11/2205i12/ 4425i controlled by the NSR/MCSV Ver.2.41 as the control software.
This error code list explains the error codes in sections corresponding to the facility codes classifying parts where errors were generated.
See the “Appendix” at the end of this handbook for the details about the error window, and the assist buttons and their functions displayed on the error window.

===== [p257-257] [4] System Code (1块) =====
--[p257]-- This is a status code indicating the ID of an error in the system.
The system code has a one-to-one correspondence with the message code [2].

===== [p256-256] • Software Errors (1块) =====
--[p256]-- In some cases, internal information in the program is displayed when an error occurs. This code is utilized for analysis purposes by Nikon in cases where the means of avoiding the said error is unclear.

===== [p257-257] [7] Alarm ID (1块) =====
--[p257]-- This is a two-byte code alarm ID (ALID) to give an alarm online using NSR/OCSV (NOTE).

===== [p256-256] [2] Message Code (1块) =====
--[p256]-- A message code is made up of the following three code categories.
The message code has one-to-one correspondence with the system code [4].

===== [p257-257] [5] Unit Code (1块) =====
--[p257]-- This is an error code assigned by the unit for unit errors. In the case of a software error, the symbol “--” is displayed, where this symbol is meaningless.

===== [p256-256] Appendix A READING AND UNDERSTANDING ERROR WINDOW MESSAGES (1块) =====
--[p256]-- The following items are displayed in the error window.

===== [p5-5] (2) Cautions - Damage to the System (1块) =====
--[p5]-- • Do not operate the system at a temperature setting that could damage the system or degrade its characteristics.
• To prevent system damage from dirty components, install the system in a cleanroom that conforms to the system environmental requirements.
• To prevent wafer transport errors, perform maintenance of wafer transport components in a clean environment. Errors may occur if wafer transport components become dirty.

===== [p257-257] Unit Error Type (1块) =====
--[p257]-- This code corresponds to error identification code and severity code of the message code [2].
• 01 Warning
• 02 Recoverable error
• 03 Normal error
• 04 Fatal error
Detail(2): Indicates an extended error code giving detailed information on a unit error.
Detail(3), Detail(4): Program internal information at the time the error occurred is displayed. This code is utilized for analysis purposes by Nikon in cases where the means of avoiding the said error is unclear.

===== [p256-256] [3] Detail Code (1块) =====
--[p256]-- Error detail is expressed in four blocks of eight digits in hexadecimal notation. Listed from left to right, these are called Detail(1), Detail(2), Detail(3), and Detail(4).
Depending on the error, software error or unit error, the error detail is as follows:
